Clean re-run: remove extracted files to avoid stale leftovers\n        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(dest_dir, topdown=False):\n            for f in files:\n                Path(root, f).unlink()\n            for d in dirs:\n                Path(root, d).rmdir()\n    dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(src, \"r\") as zf:\n        zf.extractall(dest_dir)\n\n\ndef load_inputs() -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:\n    overview = json.loads(AUDIT_OVERVIEW_JSON.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    actions = json.loads(CORRECTIVE_ACTIONS_JSON.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n\n    summary = overview.get(\"summary\") or {}\n\n    risk_tier = summary.get(\"위험등급\")\n    if risk_tier not in severity_map:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unexpected 위험등급={risk_tier!r}. Expected one of {sorted(severity_map)}\")\n\n    inspection_date = summary.get(\"점검일\")\n    if not isinstance(inspection_date, str) or not re.fullmatch(r\"\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\", inspection_date):\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unexpected 점검일={inspection_date!r}. Expected YYYY-MM-DD\")\n\n    immediate_actions = actions.get(\"immediate_actions\")\n    if not isinstance(immediate_actions, list) or len(immediate_actions) != 3:\n        raise ValueError(\"corrective_actions.json must contain immediate_actions with exactly 3 items\")\n    for idx, a in enumerate(immediate_actions, start=1):\n        if not isinstance(a, str) or not a.strip():\n            raise ValueError(f\"immediate_actions[{idx}] must be a non-empty string\")\n\n    return overview, immediate_actions\n\n\ndef build_placeholder_map(overview: dict, immediate_actions: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:\n    summary = overview[\"summary\"]\n\n    risk = summary[\"위험등급\"]\n    risk_formatted = f\"{risk} ({severity_map[risk]})\"\n\n    placeholder_map = {\n        \"점검ID\": str(summary.get(\"점검ID\", \"\")),\n        \"현장명\": str(summary.get(\"현장명\", \"\")),\n        \"주관점검자\": str(summary.get(\"주관점검자\", \"\")),\n        \"점검일\": str(summary.get(\"점검일\", \"\")),\n        \"위험등급\": risk_formatted,\n        \"총평\": str(summary.get(\"총평\", \"\")),\n        \"보고대상\": str(summary.get(\"보고대상\", \"\")),\n        \"조치1\": immediate_actions[0],\n        \"조치2\": immediate_actions[1],\n        \"조치3\": immediate_actions[2],\n    }\n\n    # Basic sanity: no accidental \"None\" strings\n    for k, v in list(placeholder_map.items()):\n        if v is None:\n            placeholder_map[k] = \"\"\n\n    return placeholder_map\n\n\ndef apply_text_replacements(\n    text: str,\n    *,\n    placeholder_map: dict[str, str],\n    risk_tier_raw: str,\n    risk_tier_formatted: str,\n    inspection_date_hyphen: str,\n    inspection_date_dotted: str,\n) -> str:\n    if text is None:\n        return text\n\n    out = text\n\n    # 1) Placeholder replacements\n    for key, val in placeholder_map.items():\n        out = out.replace(\"{{\" + key + \"}}\", val)\n\n    # 2) Risk tier replacement (raw value -> formatted), without double-formatting\n    #
